# Third-party notices

BDOBB is Copyright 2026 SecretoftheUniverse.com LLC and licensed under AGPL-3.0-only (see `LICENSE`, `LICENSING.md` and `NOTICE`).
It ships the third-party components below, each under its own license. This file is generated by `node scripts/third-party-notices.mjs`
from `pnpm-lock.yaml` (production dependencies) and `src-tauri/Cargo.lock`, `crates/pivot-core/Cargo.lock` and `crates/pivot-wasm/Cargo.lock` (normal dependencies);
`node scripts/third-party-notices.mjs --check` fails when it is out of date.

Art and fonts: the application icons (`src-tauri/icons`), the favicons and web-app icons (`public/`) and `src/assets/branding`
are original BDOBB art. Interface icons drawn from third-party artwork are credited under Icons below. The interface uses
the operating system's fonts for its UI; the PDF viewer separately bundles PDFium/Foxit fonts (BSD-3-Clause) and Liberation fonts (OFL-1.1). OpenBB and the OpenBB "BB" mark are trademarks of OpenBB Inc.,
used only to identify OpenBB connections.
Charts drawn with TradingView Lightweight Charts carry the visible credit "Rendered by TradingView".

A package marked "(no license file shipped)" publishes only its license identifier; the standard text of that license
appears below under License texts, printed for another package under the same license.

## PDF viewer fonts

Vite emits the pdfjs-dist/standard_fonts directory, including its license files.
These fonts retain their own terms and are not relicensed under AGPL.

## Icons

Interface icons drawn from third-party artwork, each under its own licence.
The application icons, favicons and `src/assets/branding` are original BDOBB art and are not listed here.

### Pivot

`src/components/PivotIcon.tsx` draws **"Pivot" by Matt Garbarino, from the Noun
Project** — <https://thenounproject.com/icon/pivot-115905/> — licensed under
Creative Commons Attribution 3.0 (CC BY 3.0),
<https://creativecommons.org/licenses/by/3.0/>.

Changes made: cropped to the artwork (the download's two lines of credit text,
which sit beneath the drawing, cannot be read at the size a title bar draws
it), and drawn in the interface's text colour instead of black. The credit is given here, in `NOTICE`, in the generated
`THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.md` (from `scripts/third-party-notices.mjs`), and in the
Pivot button's tooltip.

### Connection

The `connections` glyph in `src/components/RailIcon.tsx`, drawn on the
Connections row of the left rail, is **"connection" by Muh Syafri, from the
Noun Project** — <https://thenounproject.com/icon/connection-8239243/> —
licensed under Creative Commons Attribution 3.0 (CC BY 3.0),
<https://creativecommons.org/licenses/by/3.0/>.

Changes made: cropped to the artwork (the download's two lines of credit text,
which sit beneath the drawing, cannot be read at the size the rail draws it),
and drawn in the interface's text colour instead of black. The credit is given here, in `NOTICE`, and in the generated
`THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.md` (from `scripts/third-party-notices.mjs`).

````text
*ring* uses an "ISC" license, like BoringSSL used to use, for new code
files. See LICENSE-other-bits for the text of that license.

See LICENSE-BoringSSL for code that was sourced from BoringSSL under the
Apache 2.0 license. Some code that was sourced from BoringSSL under the ISC
license. In each case, the license info is at the top of the file.

for the license to code that was sourced from the once_cell project.
````
